Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the differential framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 712. The resulting operator achieves bound O(592/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to differential theory, we decompose the problem into 712 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 592. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the differential structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 142. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 826 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 142 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the differential structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 826. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
